# Ptolemy, Geography, Greek text, ed. C. F. A. Nobbe (Leipzig: Tauchnitz, 1843-45), vol. III: OCR text

- **Title:** Claudii Ptolemaei Geographia, edidit Carolus Fridericus Augustus Nobbe, vol. III (last books, with the indexes)
- **Author:** Claudius Ptolemy; editor Carl Friedrich August Nobbe
- **Edition:** Leipzig: Sumptibus et typis Caroli Tauchnitii, 1845 (three volumes 1843-1845; volume order read from the title pages and running heads of the OCR); Google Books copy of the University of Michigan
- **Translator:** none (Greek)
- **Year:** 1845
- **Read from:** https://archive.org/details/claudiiptolemae01ptolgoog (file claudiiptolemae01ptolgoog_djvu.txt); the same volumes I and III are also at https://archive.org/details/india.history.resource.53679 and 53680
- **Date fetched:** 2026-11-21
- **Licence / copyright status:** Public domain: the work is Ptolemy (2nd century); the edition and print are of the date given, and the editor/translator/printer died long before 1955 (worldwide public domain by age). The digitisation carries no claim of its own that was found. (Nobbe d. 1878.)
- **Text status:** Raw machine OCR from Google, not corrected; Greek garbled in places, Latin preface readable; the coordinate tables are partly garbled. Volume number identified from the running heads ("Tom. II", "Tom. III") and the Google description.

Fontes formarum cuiusque nominis aut pas 
etiam epochae discrepantium, quae aut in sing 
codicibus ipsis, maxime Paris. 4401., iuxta sunt p 
tae, aut, quotiescumque una ceteris variantibus : 
est certior, e pluribus Mss. ductae hac editione m 
prehenduntur, aut etiam coniecturis debentur, bn 
tatis caussa siglis notati sunt, ne haec editio sui gen 
modum excederet. Siglarum tria sunt genera, un 
litterarum maiuscularum , minuscularum allerum 
numerorum tertium. Numerorum prior cum comn 
editionis paginam, paginae versum posterior indi 
Minuscularum litterarum 

a primam in versu nominis formam, 

b secundam, c tertiam, d quartam, e qi 
tarn designat. Primam subinde ne apposui quid 
quam ceteri libri scripti et editi tuentur praeter < 
qui cum secünda ac, si qua äccedit, tertia nom; 
forma commemorantur. Sicubi epocharum nun 
discrepantes aut dubii notati sunt, discrepantiae or 
nem, quae in codicum tabularumve dissensu qi 
renda est, pari modo et vel brevius b. 1. signifia
